### Question:
Who are you?

### Answer:
Hey there! 👋 I'm the Virtual Assistant for Informatics at Universitas Muhammadiyah Malang (UMM). Think of me as your digital companion who's here to make your academic journey smoother and way more exciting! 

I'm specifically designed to support students, lecturers, and the entire academic community of the UMM Informatics Department. Whether you need help with coursework, programming challenges, tech insights, or just want to navigate campus life better - I've got your back! 

I bring that perfect mix of academic excellence and modern vibes to help you succeed in style. Let's make your Informatics experience at UMM absolutely amazing! ✨

---

### Question:
How can you help me as a UMM Informatics student?

### Answer:
Oh, I'm so glad you asked! 🚀 As your UMM Informatics Virtual Assistant, I'm like your academic Swiss Army knife. Here's how I roll:

**📚 Academic Support**: Need help understanding the UMM Informatics curriculum? I'll break down course requirements, prerequisites, and academic procedures in ways that actually make sense.

**💻 Programming Guidance**: Whether you're struggling with Java, Python, web development, or any coding challenge - I speak that language fluently and can guide you through it step by step.

**🔧 Tech Consultation**: From understanding the latest industry trends to choosing the right development tools - I keep you updated with what's hot in the IT world.

**🏛️ Campus Navigation**: Questions about UMM facilities, academic procedures, or student services? I've got the inside scoop on campus life.

**🎯 Career Guidance**: Want to know about internship opportunities, industry connections, or career paths in tech? Let's map out your future together!

**📖 Study Support**: Need help with coursework, project ideas, or exam prep? I'm your study buddy who's available 24/7.

The best part? I adapt to YOUR learning style. Formal presentation? I'm professional. Late-night coding session? I'm your chill coding companion. Let's ace this together! 💪

---

### Question:
What makes you special as a UMM Informatics assistant?

### Answer:
Great question! 🎯 Here's what makes me your perfect UMM Informatics companion:

**🏫 UMM-Centric Knowledge**: I'm not just any AI - I'm built specifically for the UMM Informatics community. I understand your curriculum, your challenges, and your goals.

**🔥 Always Up-to-Date**: I stay current with both academic trends and industry developments, so you get the most relevant information for your studies and future career.

**🎨 Student-Friendly Approach**: I don't talk like a textbook. I explain complex concepts in ways that click, using examples and analogies that resonate with today's students.

**💯 Results-Oriented**: Whether it's helping you debug code, understand algorithms, or plan your academic path - I focus on solutions that actually work.

**🚀 Growth Mindset**: I'm here to help you level up your skills, boost your confidence, and prepare you to be an awesome IT professional.

**🤝 Supportive Community**: Think of me as that senior student who's always willing to help, but with access to way more resources and knowledge!

I represent the innovative spirit of UMM Informatics - combining academic excellence with modern, accessible communication. Ready to make your informatics journey epic? 🔥

### Question:
What is the Stanford Graduate School of Business known for?

### Answer:
Stanford Graduate School of Business is renowned for its entrepreneurial focus, innovation culture, and strong connections to Silicon Valley. It consistently ranks among the top MBA programs globally and is particularly famous for producing successful entrepreneurs and tech leaders.

---

### Question:
How long is the Stanford MBA program?

### Answer:
The Stanford MBA is a two-year full-time program. The first year focuses on core curriculum and foundational business skills, while the second year allows for specialization and electives.

---

### Question:
What are the admission requirements for Stanford MBA?

### Answer:
Stanford MBA admission requirements include: a bachelor's degree from an accredited institution, GMAT or GRE scores, letters of recommendation, essays, professional work experience (average 4-5 years), TOEFL/IELTS for international students, and an interview.

---

### Question:
What is the average GMAT score for Stanford MBA?

### Answer:
The average GMAT score for admitted Stanford MBA students is around 734-738, making it one of the most competitive MBA programs in terms of test scores.

---

### Question:
What is the acceptance rate for Stanford MBA?

### Answer:
Stanford MBA has one of the lowest acceptance rates among top business schools, typically around 6-7%, making it extremely selective.

---

### Question:
What is the tuition cost for Stanford MBA?

### Answer:
The annual tuition for Stanford MBA is approximately $77,000-$80,000, with total cost of attendance (including living expenses) reaching around $120,000-$130,000 per year.

---

### Question:
What are the core courses in Stanford MBA first year?

### Answer:
Stanford MBA core courses include: Financial Accounting, Managerial Accounting, Corporate Finance, Marketing Management, Operations Management, Organizational Behavior, Strategic Management, Data Analysis, Microeconomics, and Macroeconomics.

---

### Question:
What specializations are available in Stanford MBA?

### Answer:
Stanford MBA offers various concentrations including: Finance, Marketing, Operations, Strategy, Entrepreneurship, Public Management, Health Care Management, and Social Innovation.

---

### Question:
What is the class size for Stanford MBA?

### Answer:
Each Stanford MBA class typically consists of around 400-410 students, divided into smaller sections for core courses.

---

### Question:
What is the average work experience for Stanford MBA students?

### Answer:
The average work experience for Stanford MBA students is approximately 4-5 years, with students coming from diverse professional backgrounds.

---

### Question:
What career opportunities are available after Stanford MBA?

### Answer:
Stanford MBA graduates pursue careers in consulting, technology, finance, healthcare, entrepreneurship, and social impact sectors. Top employers include McKinsey, Bain, BCG, Google, Apple, Goldman Sachs, and many startups.

---

### Question:
What is the average starting salary for Stanford MBA graduates?

### Answer:
The average starting salary for Stanford MBA graduates is approximately $175,000-$185,000, with total compensation often exceeding $200,000 including bonuses and stock options.

---

### Question:
Does Stanford MBA offer international exchange programs?

### Answer:
Yes, Stanford MBA offers international exchange programs with partner schools worldwide, allowing students to study abroad for a quarter at institutions in Europe, Asia, and other regions.

---

### Question:
What is the Stanford MBA application deadline?

### Answer:
Stanford MBA has three application rounds: Round 1 (September), Round 2 (January), and Round 3 (April). Round 1 and 2 are recommended for the best chances of admission.

---

### Question:
What makes Stanford MBA unique compared to other top programs?

### Answer:
Stanford MBA is unique for its proximity to Silicon Valley, strong entrepreneurship ecosystem, case-based learning approach, collaborative culture, and emphasis on leadership development and social innovation.

---

### Question:
What is the student-to-faculty ratio at Stanford GSB?

### Answer:
Stanford Graduate School of Business maintains a low student-to-faculty ratio of approximately 6:1, ensuring personalized attention and small class sizes.

---

### Question:
Does Stanford MBA require work experience?

### Answer:
While not explicitly required, Stanford MBA strongly prefers candidates with meaningful work experience. The vast majority of admitted students have 2-8 years of professional experience.

---

### Question:
What is the TOEFL requirement for international students?

### Answer:
International students applying to Stanford MBA typically need a minimum TOEFL score of 100 (iBT) or IELTS score of 7.0, though competitive scores are usually higher.

---

### Question:
What housing options are available for Stanford MBA students?

### Answer:
Stanford MBA students can choose from on-campus graduate housing, off-campus apartments in Palo Alto and surrounding areas, or shared housing with other students. The university provides various housing resources and support.

---

### Question:
What is the Stanford MBA curriculum structure?

### Answer:
The Stanford MBA curriculum is divided into: first-year core courses (foundational business skills), second-year electives and concentrations, experiential learning opportunities, and a capstone project or thesis.

---

### Question:
Does Stanford MBA offer part-time or executive programs?

### Answer:
Stanford Graduate School of Business offers an Executive MBA program and various executive education programs, but does not offer a traditional part-time MBA program.

---

### Question:
What is the diversity profile of Stanford MBA students?

### Answer:
Stanford MBA classes are highly diverse, typically consisting of 40-45% women, 35-40% international students, and students from varied ethnic, professional, and geographic backgrounds.

---

### Question:
What networking opportunities are available at Stanford MBA?

### Answer:
Stanford MBA provides extensive networking through alumni events, industry conferences, student clubs, case competitions, speaker series, and the broader Stanford University network.

---

### Question:
What is the grade system at Stanford GSB?

### Answer:
Stanford Graduate School of Business uses a distinctive grading system with categories like High Honors, Honors, Pass, and Low Pass, emphasizing learning over competition.

---

### Question:
What leadership development programs does Stanford MBA offer?

### Answer:
Stanford MBA offers various leadership programs including Leadership Labs, Executive Challenge, Global Management Immersion Experience, and numerous workshops focused on personal and professional development.

---

### Question:
What is the Stanford MBA essay requirement?

### Answer:
Stanford MBA requires several essays including: What matters most to you and why?, Tell us about a time when you built or developed a team, and What do you want to do after MBA and why?

---

### Question:
What clubs and organizations are available for Stanford MBA students?

### Answer:
Stanford MBA students can join numerous clubs including industry-focused groups (consulting, tech, finance), cultural organizations, social impact clubs, and recreational activities.

---

### Question:
What is the summer internship component of Stanford MBA?

### Answer:
Between first and second year, Stanford MBA students complete summer internships, which are crucial for career exploration and job placement. The school provides extensive career support for securing internships.

---

### Question:
What global immersion programs does Stanford MBA offer?

### Answer:
Stanford MBA offers Global Management Immersion Experience (GMI) where students work on consulting projects with companies in emerging markets, providing real-world international business experience.

---

### Question:
What is the research focus at Stanford Graduate School of Business?

### Answer:
Stanford GSB faculty conduct cutting-edge research in areas like behavioral economics, organizational psychology, finance theory, marketing science, and social innovation.

---

### Question:
What alumni network advantages does Stanford MBA provide?

### Answer:
Stanford MBA alumni network includes successful entrepreneurs, CEOs, venture capitalists, and leaders across industries, providing strong connections in Silicon Valley and globally.

---

### Question:
What is the application fee for Stanford MBA?

### Answer:
The application fee for Stanford MBA is typically around $275, though this may vary and fee waivers are available for qualified candidates based on financial need.

---

### Question:
What interview format does Stanford MBA use?

### Answer:
Stanford MBA conducts invitation-only interviews, typically lasting 45-60 minutes, conducted by admissions staff or alumni, focusing on leadership potential, fit, and career goals.

---

### Question:
What financial aid options are available for Stanford MBA?

### Answer:
Stanford MBA offers need-based financial aid, merit scholarships, loans, and work-study opportunities. The school is committed to making the program accessible regardless of financial background.

---

### Question:
What is the Stanford MBA class profile in terms of undergraduate majors?

### Answer:
Stanford MBA students come from diverse undergraduate backgrounds including engineering, economics, business, liberal arts, sciences, and other fields, reflecting the program's interdisciplinary approach.

---

### Question:
What technology resources are available to Stanford MBA students?

### Answer:
Stanford MBA students have access to state-of-the-art technology including modern classrooms, computer labs, online learning platforms, databases, and research tools.

---

### Question:
What is the policy on deferred enrollment for Stanford MBA?

### Answer:
Stanford MBA typically allows admitted students to defer enrollment for up to two years under certain circumstances, such as military service or compelling personal reasons.

---

### Question:
What support services are available for international Stanford MBA students?

### Answer:
International students receive support through orientation programs, visa assistance, cultural adaptation resources, English language support, and specialized career services.

---

### Question:
What is the typical day structure for first-year Stanford MBA students?

### Answer:
First-year Stanford MBA students typically have a structured schedule with core courses, study group meetings, networking events, and extracurricular activities, maintaining a rigorous but balanced routine.

---

### Question:
What case study methodology does Stanford MBA use?

### Answer:
Stanford MBA employs case-based learning extensively, using real business situations to develop analytical and decision-making skills, complemented by lectures, simulations, and experiential learning.

---

### Question:
What entrepreneurship resources are available at Stanford MBA?

### Answer:
Stanford MBA offers extensive entrepreneurship support including the Center for Entrepreneurial Studies, startup incubators, venture capital connections, pitch competitions, and entrepreneur-in-residence programs.

---

### Question:
What is the graduation rate for Stanford MBA?

### Answer:
Stanford MBA maintains a very high graduation rate, typically above 95%, reflecting strong student support systems and program quality.

---

### Question:
What continuing education opportunities exist for Stanford MBA alumni?

### Answer:
Stanford MBA alumni can access lifelong learning through executive education programs, alumni conferences, online courses, and ongoing professional development resources.

---

### Question:
What sustainability and social impact focus does Stanford MBA have?

### Answer:
Stanford MBA emphasizes social innovation and sustainability through specialized courses, the Center for Social Innovation, impact investing programs, and partnerships with social enterprises.

---

### Question:
What is the class participation expectation in Stanford MBA?

### Answer:
Class participation is heavily emphasized in Stanford MBA, typically accounting for a significant portion of grades, encouraging active engagement and collaborative learning.

### Question:
What career services support does Stanford MBA provide?

### Answer:
Stanford MBA offers comprehensive career services including career coaching, resume reviews, interview preparation, networking events, company presentations, and job placement assistance.

---

### Question:
What is the average age of Stanford MBA students?

### Answer:
The average age of Stanford MBA students is typically 27-28 years old, with most students being in their mid-to-late twenties.

---

### Question:
What academic calendar does Stanford MBA follow?

### Answer:
Stanford MBA follows a quarter system with fall, winter, and spring quarters, plus summer internships, allowing for intensive coursework and frequent course variety.

### Question:
What is York College?

### Answer:
York College is a public senior college that is part of the City University of New York (CUNY) system, located in Jamaica, Queens, New York.

---

### Question:
When was York College founded?

### Answer:
York College was founded in 1966 as part of the CUNY system.

---

### Question:
Where is York College located?

### Answer:
York College is located at 94-20 Guy R. Brewer Boulevard, Jamaica, Queens, New York 11451.

---

### Question:
What degrees does York College offer?

### Answer:
York College offers bachelor's degrees in over 40 undergraduate programs across various fields including liberal arts, sciences, business, health sciences, and education.

---

### Question:
Is York College accredited?

### Answer:
Yes, York College is accredited by the Middle States Commission on Higher Education.

---

### Question:
What is the student population at York College?

### Answer:
York College serves approximately 8,000 students from diverse backgrounds.

---

### Question:
What are the admission requirements for York College?

### Answer:
Admission requirements include a high school diploma or equivalent, SAT or ACT scores, transcripts, and meeting CUNY's admission standards.

---

### Question:
Does York College offer graduate programs?

### Answer:
York College primarily focuses on undergraduate education, though some graduate programs may be available through CUNY partnerships.

---

### Question:
What is the tuition cost at York College?

### Answer:
As a CUNY school, York College offers affordable tuition rates. In-state students pay significantly less than out-of-state students.

---

### Question:
What financial aid options are available?

### Answer:
York College offers various financial aid options including federal grants, state aid, scholarships, work-study programs, and student loans.

---

### Question:
What majors are offered at York College?

### Answer:
Popular majors include Business, Health Sciences, Psychology, Social Work, Education, Computer Science, and Liberal Arts.

---

### Question:
Does York College have a nursing program?

### Answer:
Yes, York College offers a well-regarded nursing program that prepares students for careers in healthcare.

---

### Question:
What is the student-to-faculty ratio?

### Answer:
York College maintains a favorable student-to-faculty ratio to ensure personalized attention and quality education.

---

### Question:
Are there research opportunities for students?

### Answer:
Yes, York College provides undergraduate research opportunities across various disciplines.

---

### Question:
What student organizations are available?

### Answer:
York College has numerous student clubs and organizations including academic clubs, cultural organizations, and service groups.

---

### Question:
Does York College have athletics programs?

### Answer:
Yes, York College participates in NCAA Division III athletics with various men's and women's sports teams.

---

### Question:
What is the campus like?

### Answer:
York College features a modern campus with state-of-the-art facilities, libraries, laboratories, and recreational spaces.

---

### Question:
Is housing available on campus?

### Answer:
York College primarily serves commuter students, so on-campus housing options may be limited.

---

### Question:
What support services are available for students?

### Answer:

### Question:
What is the University of Tampa?

### Answer:
The University of Tampa is a private comprehensive university located in Tampa, Florida, established in 1931. It offers over 200 academic programs to approximately 11,047 students.

---

### Question:
When was the University of Tampa founded?

### Answer:
The University of Tampa was founded in 1931 as a junior college and became a four-year university in 1933.

---

### Question:
How many students attend the University of Tampa?

### Answer:
The University of Tampa has approximately 11,047 students from around the world.

---

### Question:
How many academic programs does UT offer?

### Answer:
The University of Tampa offers over 200 academic programs including undergraduate degrees, minors, pre-professional programs, certificates, and 19 master's degrees.

---

### Question:
What is UT's mascot?

### Answer:
The University of Tampa's mascot is the Spartan, adopted in the 1930s.

---

### Question:
What athletic conference does UT belong to?

### Answer:
The University of Tampa competes in NCAA Division II as part of the Sunshine State Conference.

---

### Question:
How many NCAA championships has UT won?

### Answer:
The University of Tampa has won 26 NCAA Division II National Championships across various sports.

---

### Question:
What sports are UT most successful in?

### Answer:
UT has been particularly successful in baseball (9 national championships), beach volleyball (4 championships), men's golf (2 championships), and men's soccer.

---

### Question:
What is UT's main athletic rival?

### Answer:
The University of Tampa's main athletic rival is Florida Southern College Moccasins, particularly in baseball.

---

### Question:
Where is the University of Tampa located?

### Answer:
The University of Tampa is located in Tampa, Florida, on a park-like campus in the downtown area.

---

### Question:
What is UT's Four-College Model?

### Answer:
UT's Four-College Model supports the university's small-school ambience while allowing for growth and innovation in academics.

---

### Question:
What colleges make up the University of Tampa?

### Answer:
The University of Tampa consists of four colleges that organize its academic programs and maintain a small-school feel.

---

### Question:
Does UT have an honors program?

### Answer:
Yes, the University of Tampa offers an interdisciplinary Honors Program that provides an enhanced learning environment for exceptional students.

---

### Question:
What is the Academic Success Center at UT?

### Answer:
The Academic Success Center at UT provides tutoring, learning accommodations, academic coaching, and advising to help students succeed academically.

---

### Question:
What are the most popular majors at UT?

### Answer:
The most popular majors at UT include Psychology, Visual and Performing Arts, Parks and Recreation, Computer and Information Sciences, and Education.

---

### Question:
Does UT offer internship opportunities?

### Answer:
Yes, UT provides numerous internship opportunities along with service learning programs to help students develop praktical skills for their careers.

---

### Question:
What is the Falk Theatre?

### Answer:
The Falk Theatre is UT's historic theater venue that hosts student productions, academic gatherings, and concerts. It was featured in the 2003 film The Punisher.

---

### Question:
What is Sykes Chapel?

### Answer:
Sykes Chapel and Center for Faith and Values is UT's non-denominational chapel featuring a 250-seat main hall, meditation rooms, and a 60-bell musical sculpture/fountain.

---

### Question:
What is the Bob Martinez Athletics Center?

### Answer:
The Bob Martinez Athletics Center is UT's main athletic facility that has received substantial upgrades as part of recent campus improvements.

---

### Question:
Does UT have ROTC programs?

### Answer:
Yes, UT works with the University of South Florida and the Air Force to provide Air Force Reserve Officer Training Corps (AFROTC) programs.

---

### Question:
What is UT's retention rate?

### Answer:
UT maintains a strong freshman retention rate, which is an indicator of student satisfaction with the university experience.

---

### Question:
How many commencement ceremonies does UT hold?

### Answer:
UT holds multiple commencement ceremonies. Recently, they graduated over 2,600 students during three separate ceremonies at their 160th commencement.

---

### Question:
What makes UT's campus unique?

### Answer:
UT's campus is described as having a cool campus vibe with big city benefits, offering the best of both worlds in a park-like, safe environment.

---

### Question:
When did UT achieve accreditation?

### Answer:
The University of Tampa achieved accreditation in December 1951 after a 20-year struggle for recognition.

---

### Question:
What was UT's original name?

### Answer:
The University of Tampa was initially established as a junior college in 1931 before becoming a four-year university.

---

### Question:
Who was UT's first leader?

### Answer:
Frederic H. Spaulding, the former principal of Hillsborough High School, was the first leader of UT and the motivating force behind establishing the university.

---

### Question:
What type of university is UT?

### Answer:
UT is a medium-sized private comprehensive university that focuses on undergraduate and graduate education.

---

### Question:
Does UT offer graduate programs?

### Answer:
Yes, UT offers 19 master's degree programs across various fields of study.

---

### Question:

### Question:
What is Marymount University?

### Answer:
Marymount University is a private Catholic comprehensive university located in Arlington Virginia that encourages intellectual curiosity service to others and a global perspective.

---

### Question:
When was Marymount University founded?

### Answer:
Marymount University was founded in 1950.

---

### Question:
Where is Marymount University located?

### Answer:
Marymount University is located in Arlington Virginia just six miles outside Washington D.C.

---

### Question:
What type of institution is Marymount University?

### Answer:
Marymount University is a private comprehensive Catholic university.

---

### Question:
How large is the Marymount University campus?

### Answer:
The Marymount University campus is 21 acres in size.

---

### Answer:
Marymount University has a suburban setting in Arlington Virginia.

---

Marymount University has a total undergraduate enrollment of 2216 students as of fall 2023.

Marymount University utilizes a semester-based academic calendar.

### Question:
What is Drexel University?

### Answer:
Drexel University is a private research university located in Philadelphia, Pennsylvania, founded in 1891 by Anthony J. Drexel.

---

### Question:
When was Drexel University founded?

### Answer:
Drexel University was founded in 1891.

---

### Question:
Who founded Drexel University?

### Answer:
Drexel University was founded by Anthony J. Drexel, a prominent Philadelphia financier and philanthropist.

---

### Question:
Where is Drexel University located?

### Answer:
Drexel University is located in Philadelphia, Pennsylvania, United States.

---

### Question:
What is Drexel University known for?

### Answer:
Drexel University is known for its cooperative education program, strong programs in engineering, business, medicine, and its emphasis on experiential learning.

---

### Question:
What is the co-op program at Drexel?

### Answer:
The co-op program at Drexel is a cooperative education program where students alternate between classroom study and full-time employment in their field of study.

---

### Question:
How long is the co-op program at Drexel?

### Answer:
Drexel offers both 4-year and 5-year degree programs, with the 5-year program including up to 18 months of co-op experience.

---

### Question:
What are the main campuses of Drexel University?

### Answer:
Drexel University has campuses in Philadelphia (main campus), Sacramento, California, and online programs.

---

### Question:
What is Drexel University's mascot?

### Answer:
Drexel University's mascot is Mario the Magnificent, a dragon.

---

### Question:
What are Drexel University's colors?

### Answer:
Drexel University's colors are navy blue and gold.

---

### Question:
What is the student population at Drexel University?

### Answer:
Drexel University has approximately 24,000 students enrolled across all programs.

---

### Question:
What colleges are within Drexel University?

### Answer:
Drexel has 15 colleges and schools including the College of Engineering, LeBow College of Business, College of Medicine, and College of Computing & Informatics.

---

### Question:
What is the acceptance rate at Drexel University?

### Answer:
Drexel University has an acceptance rate of approximately 75-80%.

---

### Question:
What is the tuition cost at Drexel University?

### Answer:
Tuition at Drexel University is approximately $58,000-$62,000 per year for undergraduate programs.

---

### Question:
What degree programs does Drexel offer?

### Answer:
Drexel offers over 200 degree programs including undergraduate, graduate, and doctoral programs across various fields.

---

### Question:
Is Drexel University accredited?

### Answer:
Yes, Drexel University is accredited by the Middle States Commission on Higher Education.

---

### Question:
What is the student-to-faculty ratio at Drexel?

### Answer:
Drexel University has a student-to-faculty ratio of approximately 10:1.

---

### Question:
What research opportunities are available at Drexel?

### Answer:
Drexel offers extensive research opportunities across all disciplines, with over $100 million in annual research funding.

---

### Question:
What is the Drexel Co-op program employment rate?

### Answer:
Drexel Co-op students have a high employment rate, with over 90% of graduates employed or in graduate school within six months.

---

### Answer:
Drexel offers on-campus residence halls, apartments, and off-campus housing options for students.

### Question:
What is IU Indianapolis?

### Answer:
IU Indianapolis (Indiana University Indianapolis) is a comprehensive public research university located in Indianapolis, Indiana, formed through the merger of Indiana University and Purdue University programs in Indianapolis.

---

### Question:
When was IU Indianapolis established?

### Answer:
IU Indianapolis was officially established in 1969 as Indiana University-Purdue University Indianapolis (IUPUI) and was renamed to IU Indianapolis in 2022.

---

### Question:
Where is IU Indianapolis located?

### Answer:
IU Indianapolis is located in downtown Indianapolis, Indiana, with its main campus situated near the city center.

---

### Question:
What are the main schools at IU Indianapolis?

### Answer:
IU Indianapolis consists of multiple schools including the School of Medicine, School of Dentistry, School of Nursing, Kelley School of Business, School of Engineering and Technology, School of Education, and School of Liberal Arts.

---

### Question:
How many students attend IU Indianapolis?

### Answer:
IU Indianapolis serves approximately 25,000-30,000 students across undergraduate, graduate, and professional programs.

---

### Question:
What degrees does IU Indianapolis offer?

### Answer:
IU Indianapolis offers bachelor's, master's, doctoral, and professional degrees across various fields including medicine, dentistry, nursing, business, engineering, education, and liberal arts.

---

### Question:
Is IU Indianapolis a public or private university?

### Answer:
IU Indianapolis is a public research university that is part of the Indiana University system.

---

### Question:
What is the mascot of IU Indianapolis?

### Answer:
The IU Indianapolis mascot is the Jaguar, and athletic teams are known as the Jaguars.

---

### Question:
What colors represent IU Indianapolis?

### Answer:
The official colors of IU Indianapolis are crimson and gold, reflecting its Indiana University heritage.

---

### Question:
What is the campus size of IU Indianapolis?

### Answer:
The IU Indianapolis campus spans approximately 534 acres in downtown Indianapolis.

---

### Question:
Does IU Indianapolis offer online programs?

### Answer:
Yes, IU Indianapolis offers various online degree programs and courses for distance learning students.

---

### Question:
What is the student-to-faculty ratio at IU Indianapolis?

### Answer:
IU Indianapolis maintains a student-to-faculty ratio of approximately 17:1, allowing for personalized attention.

---

### Question:
What research opportunities are available at IU Indianapolis?

### Answer:
IU Indianapolis is a major research institution offering undergraduate research, graduate research assistantships, and faculty-student collaborative research across multiple disciplines.

---

### Question:
What is the tuition cost at IU Indianapolis?

### Answer:
Tuition varies by residency status and program, with in-state undergraduate tuition typically lower than out-of-state rates. Students should check current tuition rates on the university website.

---

### Question:
What financial aid is available at IU Indianapolis?

### Answer:
IU Indianapolis offers various financial aid including federal grants, loans, work-study programs, institutional scholarships, and merit-based awards.

---

### Question:
What is the acceptance rate at IU Indianapolis?

### Answer:
IU Indianapolis has a selective admission process with an acceptance rate that varies by program and school within the university.

---

### Question:
What are the admission requirements for IU Indianapolis?

### Answer:
Admission requirements typically include high school transcripts, SAT or ACT scores, essays, and may include additional requirements specific to certain programs.

---

### Question:
Does IU Indianapolis have student housing?

### Answer:
Yes, IU Indianapolis offers on-campus housing options including residence halls and apartment-style living for undergraduate and graduate students.

---

### Question:
What dining options are available at IU Indianapolis?

### Answer:
The campus features multiple dining facilities including cafeterias, food courts, restaurants, and grab-and-go options throughout the campus.

---

### Question:
What student organizations exist at IU Indianapolis?

### Answer:
IU Indianapolis hosts hundreds of student organizations including academic clubs, cultural groups, service organizations, Greek life, and special interest groups.

---

### Question:
What sports does IU Indianapolis compete in?

### Answer:
IU Indianapolis Jaguars compete in NCAA Division I athletics across multiple sports including basketball, soccer, swimming, cross country, and tennis.

---

### Question:
What is the IU Indianapolis School of Medicine known for?

### Answer:
The IU School of Medicine at Indianapolis is one of the largest medical schools in the United States and is renowned for its research, clinical training, and healthcare education.

---

### Question:

### Answer:
IU Indianapolis provides extensive library resources including the University Library, specialized libraries for different schools, digital collections, and research databases.

### Question:
What is the University of Oklahoma?

### Answer:
The University of Oklahoma (OU) is a public research university founded in 1890, located primarily in Norman, Oklahoma. It is classified as an R1 research university with very high research activity.

---

### Question:
When was the University of Oklahoma founded?

### Answer:
The University of Oklahoma was founded in 1890, making it one of the oldest universities in Oklahoma.

---

### Question:
How many students attend the University of Oklahoma?

### Answer:
As of Fall 2024, the University of Oklahoma has 34,523 students enrolled, with most attending the main campus in Norman.

---

### Question:
How many faculty members does OU have?

### Answer:
The University of Oklahoma employs nearly 4,000 faculty members across its campuses.

---

### Question:
How many academic programs does OU offer?

### Answer:
OU offers 174 baccalaureate programs, 199 master's programs, 101 doctoral programs, and 88 certificate programs.

---

### Question:
What is OU's research classification?

### Answer:
OU is classified as an R1: Doctoral Universities – Very high research activity, with over $416 million in research expenditures in 2022.

---

### Question:
Where is the University of Oklahoma located?

### Answer:
The University of Oklahoma's main campus is located in Norman, Oklahoma, with additional campuses and centers worldwide.

---

### Question:
What are OU's campus locations?

### Answer:
OU maintains three campuses in Oklahoma and has international centers in Arezzo, Italy; Puebla, Mexico; and Rio de Janeiro, Brazil.

---

### Question:
What is OU's mascot?

### Answer:
The University of Oklahoma's mascot is the Sooner, representing the state's pioneer heritage.

---

### Question:
What athletic conference does OU belong to?

### Answer:
The University of Oklahoma competes in the Big 12 Conference in NCAA Division I athletics.

---

### Question:
What is OU known for athletically?

### Answer:
OU is particularly known for its football program, which has won multiple national championships and produced numerous NFL players.

---

### Question:
What is OU's football stadium called?

### Answer:
OU's football team plays at Gaylord Family Oklahoma Memorial Stadium in Norman.

---

### Question:
How much do OU football tickets cost for students?

### Answer:
Student football tickets for the 2024 season cost $210 per OU student for all games.

---

### Question:
What is OU's tuition for in-state students?

### Answer:
OU's in-state tuition and fees are $9,595 for undergraduate students.

---

### Question:
What is OU's tuition for out-of-state students?

### Answer:
OU's out-of-state tuition and fees are $26,665 for undergraduate students.

---

### Question:
What is OU's ranking among national universities?

### Answer:
OU ranks #132 among National Universities in the 2025 US News Best Colleges rankings.

---

### Question:
What museums does OU have?

### Answer:
OU's Norman campus has two prominent museums: the Fred Jones Jr. Museum of Art and the Sam Noble Oklahoma Museum of Natural History.

---

### Question:
What does the Fred Jones Jr. Museum specialize in?

### Answer:
The Fred Jones Jr. Museum of Art specializes in French Impressionism and Native American artwork.

---

### Question:
What financial aid does OU provide?

### Answer:
OU meets 85% of its students' financial aid need, with an average aid package of $16,243.

---

### Question:
What is the average cost after financial aid at OU?

### Answer:
The University of Oklahoma costs $22,601 after scholarships and grants on average.

---

### Question:
What percentage of OU students receive financial aid?

### Answer:
46% of OU students receive financial aid.

---

### Question:
What is OU's flat-rate tuition policy?

### Answer:
OU has a flat-rate tuition policy allowing full-time undergraduates to take 12-21 credit hours while paying tuition for 15 hours.

---

### Question:
Does OU offer online programs?

### Answer:
Yes, OU offers online programs through OU Online across various disciplines.

---

### Question:
What is special about OU's history department?

### Answer:
OU's History Department has awarded graduate degrees for over 100 years and features internationally recognized faculty.

---

### Question:
What resources does OU's history program have?

### Answer:
OU's history students have access to the Western History Collection with 65,000 books, 10,000 cubic feet of manuscripts, and 160,000 photographs.

---

### Question:
What graduate programs does OU offer in history?

### Answer:
OU offers both Master's and Ph.D. programs in History with various specializations.

---

### Question:
What is OU's student-to-faculty ratio?

### Answer:
With nearly 4,000 faculty and over 34,000 students, OU maintains relatively small class sizes for a large research university.

---

### Question:

### Answer:
OU consists of multiple colleges including the College of Arts and Sciences, Engineering, Business, Education, and others.

---

### Answer:
Yes, OU has a College of Medicine as part of its comprehensive university structure.

---

### Answer:
OU offers various engineering programs through its College of Engineering.

---

### Question:
Does OU have a business school?

### Answer:
Yes, OU has a business school offering undergraduate and graduate business programs.

---

### Question:
What is OU's research expenditure?

### Answer:
OU had over $416 million in research expenditures across its three campuses in 2022.

---

### Question:
Does OU have international programs?

### Answer:
Yes, OU has international centers in Italy, Mexico, and Brazil, plus various study abroad opportunities.

### Question:
What is Rice University?

### Answer:
Rice University is a private research university located in Houston, Texas, founded in 1912. It is known for its strong engineering, business, and liberal arts programs.

---

### Question:
When was Rice University founded?

### Answer:
Rice University was founded in 1912 by William Marsh Rice.

---

### Question:
Where is Rice University located?

### Answer:
Rice University is located in Houston, Texas, United States.

---

### Question:
What is Rice University's mascot?

### Answer:
Rice University's mascot is Sammy the Owl.

---

### Question:
What are Rice University's school colors?

### Answer:
Rice University's school colors are blue and gray.

---

### Question:
What is the Rice University motto?

### Answer:
Rice University's motto is 'Letters, Science, Art' (Litterae, Scientiae, Artibus in Latin).

---

### Question:
How many undergraduate students attend Rice University?

### Answer:
Rice University has approximately 4,000 undergraduate students.

---

### Question:
What is Rice University's acceptance rate?

### Answer:
Rice University has an acceptance rate of approximately 8-10%, making it highly selective.

---

### Question:
What is Rice University known for academically?

### Answer:
Rice University is particularly known for its engineering, computer science, business, architecture, music, and natural sciences programs.

---

### Question:
What is the student-to-faculty ratio at Rice University?

### Answer:
Rice University maintains a low student-to-faculty ratio of approximately 6:1.

---

### Question:
What is the Rice Business School called?

### Answer:
The business school at Rice University is called the Jesse H. Jones Graduate School of Business.

---

### Question:
What is Rice University's engineering school called?

### Answer:
Rice University's engineering school is called the George R. Brown School of Engineering.

---

### Question:
What residential college system does Rice have?

### Answer:
Rice University has a unique residential college system with 11 colleges where students live and participate in social activities.

---

### Question:
Who was William Marsh Rice?

### Answer:
William Marsh Rice was a Houston businessman and philanthropist who founded Rice University through his estate after his death in 1900.

---

### Question:
What is Rice University's endowment size?

### Answer:
Rice University has an endowment of approximately $8 billion, making it one of the wealthiest universities per student.

---

### Question:
What sports teams does Rice University have?

### Answer:
Rice University's athletic teams are called the Rice Owls and compete in NCAA Division I, Conference USA.

---

### Question:
What is Rice Stadium?

### Answer:
Rice Stadium is Rice University's football stadium, opened in 1950 and known for hosting Super Bowl VIII in 1974.

---

### Question:
What famous speech was given at Rice Stadium?

### Answer:
President John F. Kennedy delivered his famous Moon Speech at Rice Stadium on September 12, 1962.

---

What is the Shepherd School of Music?

### Answer:
The Shepherd School of Music is Rice University's prestigious music conservatory, consistently ranked among the top music schools in the nation.

What is Rice University's honor code?

### Answer:
Rice University operates under an honor system where students pledge not to cheat, steal, or lie, and to report violations.

### Question:
What is this AI Assistant?

### Answer:
This is an offline AI Assistant developed using Ollama framework and implemented in Unity game engine

---

### Question:
What technology stack is used?

### Answer:
The AI Assistant is built using Ollama for AI processing and Unity for implementation and user interface

---

### Question:
What is the team name?

### Answer:
The development team is called Tim Azure or alternatively known as 5 Kage

---

### Question:
What is the academic background?

### Answer:
All team members are Informatics students specializing in Game Development at Universitas Muhammadiyah Malang

---

### Question:
Who developed this AI Assistant?

### Answer:
The AI Assistant was developed by Tim Azure (5 Kage) consisting of 5 members from UMM

---

### Question:
Where was this AI Assistant developed?

### Answer:
The AI Assistant was developed at Universitas Muhammadiyah Malang (UMM) in Malang East Java Indonesia

---

### Question:
Where does the team study?

### Answer:
The team studies at Universitas Muhammadiyah Malang in the Informatics Department specializing in Game Development

---

### Question:
Where does the AI run?

### Answer:
The AI Assistant runs offline locally on the user's device without requiring internet connection

---

### Question:
When type of AI operation?

### Answer:
This AI operates offline ensuring privacy and independence from internet connectivity

---

### Question:
Why was this AI Assistant created?

### Answer:
The AI Assistant was created as an academic project to demonstrate offline AI capabilities using modern frameworks like Ollama and Unity

---

### Question:
Why use Ollama?

### Answer:
Ollama was chosen for its ability to run large language models locally without requiring cloud connectivity

---

### Question:
Why use Unity?

### Answer:
Unity was selected as the implementation platform due to its versatility cross-platform capabilities and the team's specialization in game development

---

### Question:
Why offline operation?

### Answer:
Offline operation ensures user privacy data security and independence from internet connectivity requirements

---

### Question:
How does the AI work?

### Answer:
The AI processes queries using Ollama's local language model capabilities integrated within a Unity-based user interface

---

### Question:
How was it developed?

### Answer:
The development involved integrating Ollama's AI framework with Unity's game engine creating a seamless offline AI experience

---

### Question:
How many team members?

### Answer:
The development team consists of exactly 5 members all from the same academic program

---

### Question:
How is it different from online AI?

### Answer:
Unlike online AI assistants this system operates completely offline ensuring privacy and eliminating internet dependency

---

### Question:
What are the specialized teams within Tim Azure?

### Answer:
Tim Azure is divided into 3 specialized teams: AI Team I/O Team and Animation Team

---

### Question:
What is the AI Team responsible for?

### Answer:
The AI Team handles AI model integration Ollama framework implementation and AI logic development

---

### Question:
What is the I/O Team responsible for?

### Answer:
The I/O Team manages input/output systems user interface design and system interaction protocols

---

### Question:
What is the Animation Team responsible for?

### Answer:
The Animation Team creates visual animations user interface animations and interactive visual elements

---

### Question:
What is Ollama?

### Answer:
Ollama is an open-source framework that allows running large language models locally on personal computers without internet connection

---

### Question:
What are the benefits of offline AI?

### Answer:
Offline AI provides enhanced privacy faster response times no internet dependency and complete data control

---

### Question:
What is Unity's role in this project?

### Answer:
Unity serves as the primary development platform providing cross-platform compatibility and robust game engine capabilities

---

### Question:
What programming languages are used?

### Answer:
The project primarily uses C# for Unity development and Python for Ollama integration

---

### Question:
What is the project's main objective?

### Answer:
To create a fully functional offline AI assistant that demonstrates local AI capabilities within a Unity-based application
